  42:  */
  43: @Slf4j
  44: @Component
  45: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  46: public class Ec2ZabbixMatcher {
  47: 
  48:     public static final String BASIS_INSTANCE_ID = "INSTANCE_ID_EXACT";
  49:     public static final String BASIS_IP = "IP_EXACT";
  50:     /** Zabbix ?몄뒪???쒓렇濡?紐낆떆 ?곌껐?섎뒗 ??沅뚯옣 ?⑤낫????臾몄꽌?????. */
  51:     static final String TAG_INSTANCE_ID = "aws.instanceId";
  52: 
  53:     private static final Duration CACHE_TTL = Duration.ofMinutes(5);
  54:     private static final SourceRef SRC = SourceRef.DEFAULT;
  55: 
  56:     private final InventoryPort inventoryPort;
  57:     private final AtomicReference<Snapshot> cache = new AtomicReference<>();
  58: 
  59:     /** 留ㅼ묶 寃곌낵 ??basis쨌?먯젙 ?쒓컖??蹂댁〈?쒕떎("???곌껐?먮굹"???붾㈃???듯븷 ???덇쾶). */
  60:     public record Match(String hostid, String hostName, String basis, Long lastCheckEpoch, Instant matchedAt) {
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     /**
  64:      * agent ?섏쭛???좎꽑?쒓? ??而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?먯젙??freshness ?꾨줉??LAST_CHECK 湲곕컲, MetricPort ?몄텧 ?놁쓬).
  65:      */
  66:     public boolean isFresh(Match match, Duration tolerance) {
  67:         return match.lastCheckEpoch() != null
  68:                 && Instant.ofEpochSecond(match.lastCheckEpoch()).isAfter(Instant.now().minus(tolerance));
  69:     }
  70: 
  71:     /**
  72:      * ?⑥씪 ?몄뒪?댁뒪 留ㅼ묶. IP 異뺤? "?몄뒪?댁뒪媛 ?뺥솗?????몄뒪?몃쭔 ?뚮┝"???붽뎄?쒕떎.
  73:      * ?좑툘 ?④굔 ?먯젙? ?몄뒪?멤넂?몄뒪?댁뒪 ??갑???좎씪?깃퉴吏??紐?蹂몃떎(?꾩껜 ?몄뒪?댁뒪 紐⑸줉???놁쑝誘濡? ??     * INSTANCE_ID ?곗꽑?쒖쐞媛 ?대? 蹂댁셿?쒕떎. 臾몄꽌?붾맂 1李??쒓퀎.
  74:      */
  75:     public Optional<Match> matchFor(Instance instance) {
  76:         Snapshot snapshot = hosts();
  77:         if (snapshot == null || instance == null) {
  78:             return Optional.empty();
  79:         }
  80:         // 1?쒖쐞: 紐낆떆 ?앸퀎??        AgentHost byId = snapshot.byInstanceId().get(instance.instanceId());
  81:         if (byId != null) {
  82:             return Optional.of(match(byId, BASIS_INSTANCE_ID));
  83:         }
  84:         // 2?쒖쐞: IP ??ENI ?꾩껜 二쇱냼濡??좎씪 ?몄뒪?몃쭔
  85:         Set<AgentHost> hits = new LinkedHashSet<>();
  86:         for (String address : instanceAddresses(instance)) {
  87:             List<AgentHost> candidates = snapshot.byAddress().get(address);
  88:             if (candidates != null) {
  89:                 hits.addAll(candidates);
  90:             }
  91:         }
  92:         if (hits.size() == 1) {
  93:             return Optional.of(match(hits.iterator().next(), BASIS_IP));
  94:         }
  95:         if (hits.size() > 1) {
  96:             log.debug("ec2 {} matches {} zabbix hosts by ip ??not linking", instance.instanceId(), hits.size());
  97:         }
  98:         return Optional.empty();
  99:     }
 100: 
 101:     /** EC2??留ㅼ묶 ?꾨낫 二쇱냼 ?꾩껜 ??primary ??ENI??secondary private IP쨌?곌껐 public IP ?ы븿(肄붾뜳??吏??. */
 102:     static Set<String> instanceAddresses(Instance instance) {
 103:         Set<String> addresses = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 104:         addIfPresent(addresses, instance.privateIpAddress());
 105:         addIfPresent(addresses, instance.publicIpAddress());
 106:         for (var eni : instance.networkInterfaces()) {
 107:             for (var ip : eni.privateIpAddresses()) {
 108:                 addIfPresent(addresses, ip.privateIpAddress());
 109:                 if (ip.association() != null) {
 110:                     addIfPresent(addresses, ip.association().publicIp());
 111:                 }
 112:             }
 113:         }
 114:         return addresses;
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     private static void addIfPresent(Set<String> into, String address) {
 118:         if (address != null && !address.isBlank()) {
 119:             into.add(address.trim());
 120:         }
 121:     }
 122: 
 123:     private Match match(AgentHost host, String basis) {
 124:         return new Match(host.hostid(), host.name(), basis, host.lastCheckEpoch(), Instant.now());
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     // ===== Zabbix ?몄뒪??罹먯떆 =====
 128: 
 129:     record AgentHost(String hostid, String name, Set<String> addresses, Long lastCheckEpoch) {
 130:     }
 131: 
 132:     private record Snapshot(Instant fetchedAt, Map<String, List<AgentHost>> byAddress,
 133:             Map<String, AgentHost> byInstanceId) {
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     /**
 137:      * 罹먯떆 濡쒕뵫 ??synchronized濡?single-flight: 媛쒖슂??由ъ쟾 蹂묐젹 ?붿껌??肄쒕뱶 罹먯떆?먯꽌 ?숈떆??Zabbix ?꾩껜
 138:      * ?몄뒪?몃? N踰?議고쉶?섎뒗 寃껋쓣 留됰뒗??肄붾뜳??짠7). TTL ??寃쎈줈???좉툑 鍮꾩슜留?移섎Ⅸ??
 139:      */
 140:     private synchronized Snapshot hosts() {
 141:         Snapshot current = cache.get();
 142:         if (current != null && current.fetchedAt().isAfter(Instant.now().minus(CACHE_TTL))) {
 143:             return current;
 144:         }
 145:         try {
 146:             List<InventoryHost> hosts = inventoryPort.listHosts(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 147:                     new HostQuery(null, null, Set.of(HostInclude.TAGS, HostInclude.LAST_CHECK)));
 148:             Map<String, List<AgentHost>> byAddress = new HashMap<>();
 149:             Map<String, AgentHost> byInstanceId = new HashMap<>();
 150:             Set<String> ambiguousInstanceIds = new HashSet<>();
 151:             for (InventoryHost host : hosts) {
 152:                 Set<String> addresses = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 153:                 for (HostNetworkInterface iface : host.interfaces()) {
 154:                     // ?뵶 AGENT ?명꽣?섏씠?ㅻ쭔 ??SNMP/IPMI 二쇱냼濡?"agent ?덉쓬" ?먯젙?섎㈃ ?깅뒫 吏?쒓? ?녿뒗??                    //    ?덈떎怨?留먰븯???덉씠 ?쒕떎(肄붾뜳??吏??.
 155:                     // ?뵶 useip=0(DNS ?묒냽) ?명꽣?섏씠?ㅻ뒗 ?쒖쇅 ??洹??몄뒪?몄쓽 ip ?꾨뱶???묒냽?????곕뒗 ?ㅼ젙媛믪씠??                    //    EC2 二쇱냼? ?곗뿰??媛숈븘??洹쇨굅媛 ?꾨땲??肄붾뜳??寃??짠2). useIp 誘몄긽(null)? ?ы븿.
 156:                     if (iface.kind() == InterfaceKind.AGENT && !Boolean.FALSE.equals(iface.useIp())) {
 157:                         addIfPresent(addresses, iface.address());
 158:                     }
 159:                 }
 160:                 if (addresses.isEmpty()) {
 161:                     continue;
 162:                 }
 163:                 AgentHost agentHost = new AgentHost(host.ref().nativeId(), host.name(), addresses, host.lastCheckEpoch());
 164:                 for (String address : addresses) {
 165:                     byAddress.computeIfAbsent(address, k -> new java.util.ArrayList<>()).add(agentHost);
 166:                 }
 167:                 for (String instanceId : instanceIdClaims(host)) {
 168:                     // 媛숈? instanceId瑜????몄뒪?멸? 二쇱옣?섎㈃ ????踰꾨┛?????좎씪??洹쒖튃.
 169:                     if (byInstanceId.put(instanceId, agentHost) != null) {
 170:                         ambiguousInstanceIds.add(instanceId);
 171:                     }
 172:                 }
 173:             }
 174:             ambiguousInstanceIds.forEach(byInstanceId::remove);
 175:             Snapshot fresh = new Snapshot(Instant.now(), byAddress, byInstanceId);
 176:             cache.set(fresh);
 177:             return fresh;
 178:         } catch (Exception e) {
 179:             log.warn("zabbix host fetch for ec2 matching failed: {}", e.getMessage());
 180:             // ?뵶 fail-closed ??留뚮즺 罹먯떆瑜??ъ궗?⑺븯硫?IP ?ы븷?뮤룻샇?ㅽ듃 ??젣 ??Zabbix ?μ븷媛 ?댁뼱吏???            //    ?됰슧???몄뒪?몄쓽 CPU/硫붾え由щ? EC2??遺숈뿬 蹂댁뿬以??肄붾뜳??寃??짠1). 誘몃ℓ移???CW ?대갚???덉쟾.
 181:             cache.set(null);
 182:             return null;
 183:         }
 184:     }
 185: 
 186:     /** ?몄뒪?멸? 二쇱옣?섎뒗 instanceId ???몄뒪?몃챸??instanceId ?뺤떇?닿굅?? aws.instanceId ?쒓렇. */
 187:     private static Set<String> instanceIdClaims(InventoryHost host) {
 188:         Set<String> claims = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 189:         if (host.name() != null && host.name().startsWith("i-")) {
 190:             claims.add(host.name().trim());
 191:         }
 192:         host.tags().stream()
 193:                 .filter(tag -> TAG_INSTANCE_ID.equals(tag.name()))
 194:                 .map(tag -> tag.value())
 195:                 .filter(v -> v != null && !v.isBlank())
 196:                 .forEach(v -> claims.add(v.trim()));
 197:         return claims;
 198:     }
 199: }

  43: 
  44:     /** CW 吏???뺤쓽 ??EC2 湲곕낯 紐⑤땲?곕쭅(5遺? 臾대즺)??議댁옱?섎뒗 寃껊쭔. */
  45:     private static final List<MetricSpec> CW_METRICS = List.of(
  46:             new MetricSpec("cpu", "CPUUtilization", Statistic.AVERAGE),
  47:             new MetricSpec("networkIn", "NetworkIn", Statistic.AVERAGE),
  48:             new MetricSpec("networkOut", "NetworkOut", Statistic.AVERAGE),
  49:             new MetricSpec("diskReadBytes", "DiskReadBytes", Statistic.AVERAGE),
  50:             new MetricSpec("diskWriteBytes", "DiskWriteBytes", Statistic.AVERAGE));
  51:     /** ?묐떟 series ???꾩껜 ???대뒓 ?뚯뒪?????뗭? 怨좎젙(FE媛 ??議댁옱濡?遺꾧린?섏? ?딄쾶). */
  52:     private static final List<String> ALL_KEYS =
  53:             List.of("cpu", "memoryUsage", "networkIn", "networkOut", "diskReadBytes", "diskWriteBytes");
  54: 
  55:     private static final String NAMESPACE = "AWS/EC2";
  56:     private static final int MAX_DATAPOINTS = 1440;
  57:     /** agent ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠????留덉?留??ъ씤?멸? ???덉씠?댁빞 "?좎꽑"?대떎(?섏쭛 吏?걔룹젙吏 媛먯?). */
  58:     private static final Duration AGENT_FRESHNESS = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  59:     private static final SourceRef SRC = SourceRef.DEFAULT;
  60: 
  61:     private final AwsReadOnlyGateway aws;
  62:     private final CloudWatchAvailability availability;
  63:     private final Ec2ZabbixMatcher matcher;
  64:     private final MetricPort metricPort;
  65: 
  66:     /**
  67:      * ?몄뒪?댁뒪 1????깅뒫 ?쒓퀎?? from/to??epoch seconds, 0?대㈃ 理쒓렐 24?쒓컙.
  68:      * ?ㅽ뙣 紐⑤뱶???꾨? 200 + 鍮?series?????듯빀 ??꾨씪?몄뿉???깅뒫 異뺣쭔 議곗슜??鍮좎쭊??
  69:      */
  70:     public Map<String, Object> metrics(String instanceId, String region, long fromEpoch, long toEpoch) {
  71:         Instant now = Instant.now();
  72:         Instant to = toEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(toEpoch) : now;
  73:         Instant from = fromEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(fromEpoch) : to.minus(Duration.ofDays(1));
  74: 
  75:         // ?? 1) Zabbix agent 寃쎈줈 ??留ㅼ묶 + ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠???듦낵 ??CW瑜?遺瑜댁? ?딅뒗???꾨━?곗뼱 ?덉빟)
  76:         Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = findMatch(instanceId, region);
  77:         if (match.isPresent()) {
  78:             Map<String, Object> agent = tryAgentSeries(match.get(), from, to, now);
  79:             if (agent != null) {
  80:                 return agent;
  81:             }
  82:         }
  83: 
  84:         // ?? 2) CloudWatch 寃쎈줈(?꾪뻾) ??留ㅼ묶?먮뒗???ш린濡??붾떎硫?紐낆떆??fallback?대떎
  85:         Map<String, Object> result = cloudWatchSeries(instanceId, region, from, to, now);
  86:         @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  87:         Map<String, Object> collection = (Map<String, Object>) result.get("collection");
  88:         collection.put("source", "cloudwatch");
  89:         collection.put("fallbackUsed", match.isPresent());
  90:         match.ifPresent(m -> collection.put("matchBasis", m.basis()));
  91:         return result;
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     private Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> findMatch(String instanceId, String region) {
  95:         try {
  96:             Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
  97:             return matcher.matchFor(instance);
  98:         } catch (Exception e) {
  99:             log.debug("ec2 lookup for matching failed ({}): {}", instanceId, e.getMessage());
 100:             return Optional.empty();
 101:         }
 102:     }
 103: 
 104:     /**
 105:      * agent ?쒓퀎????CPU util(%)쨌硫붾え由?pused(%). 1李?踰붿쐞: ?⑥씪 ?쒕━利??뺤젙 吏?쒕쭔
 106:      * (?붿뒪???ъ슜瑜졖룸꽕?몄썙?щ뒗 ?뚰떚???명꽣?섏씠???ㅼ감?먯씠??2李????ㅺ퀎 짠4).
 107:      *
 108:      * @return ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠??????議댁옱 + ?좎꽑) 誘몃떖?대㈃ null ???몄텧遺媛 CW fallback.
 109:      */
 110:     private Map<String, Object> tryAgentSeries(Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match match, Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 111:         try {
 112:             EntityRef entity = EntityRef.ofDefault(match.hostid());
 113:             TimeRange range = new TimeRange(from.getEpochSecond(), to.getEpochSecond());
 114:             // ?뵶 ?쒖뒪??而⑦뀓?ㅽ듃 ???대씪?곕뱶 ?붾㈃? awsResources.view濡?寃뚯씠?몃릺硫? ?ㅻ깄??異뺢낵 ?숈씪?섍쾶
 115:             //    ?쒖뒪???섏쭛 ?곗씠?곕? ?뚮퉬?쒕떎(?ъ슜?먮퀎 Zabbix ?몄뀡???쒖슦硫?罹먯떆쨌沅뚰븳???쏀엺????짠8).
 116:             List<Series> cpu = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 117:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.CPU, "util"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 118:             List<Series> memory = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 119:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.MEMORY, "pused"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 120: 
 121:             // ?뵶 吏?쒕퀎濡?媛곴컖 ?좎꽑?댁빞 ?쒕떎 ??max()濡??⑹튂硫?CPU媛 3?쒓컙 ?꾩씠?대룄 硫붾え由ш? 理쒖떊?대㈃
 122:             //    ?듦낵??stale CPU瑜?agent ?뚯뒪濡??대낫?몃떎(肄붾뜳??寃??짠3). ?먯젙 ???= ?묐떟 ???泥?Series).
 123:             if (!fresh(cpu, now) || !fresh(memory, now)) {
 124:                 log.debug("agent series quality gate failed for host {}", match.hostid());
 125:                 return null;
 126:             }
 127:             long latestEpoch = Math.min(latestEpoch(cpu), latestEpoch(memory));
 128: 
 129:             Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 130:             series.put("cpu", toPoints(cpu));
 131:             series.put("memoryUsage", toPoints(memory));
 132:             Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 133:             collection.put("state", "ok");
 134:             collection.put("reason", null);
 135:             collection.put("source", "zabbix-agent");
 136:             collection.put("fallbackUsed", false);
 137:             collection.put("matchBasis", match.basis());
 138:             collection.put("zabbixHostid", match.hostid());
 139:             collection.put("zabbixHostName", match.hostName());
 140:             collection.put("observedAt", Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).toString());
 141:             return Map.of("items", series, "collection", collection);
 142:         } catch (Exception e) {
 143:             // Zabbix ?μ븷??CW fallback ?ъ쑀????二쎌? ?딅뒗??
 144:             log.warn("agent series fetch failed for host {}: {}", match.hostid(), e.getMessage());
 145:             return null;
 146:         }
 147:     }
 148: 
 149:     private Map<String, Object> cloudWatchSeries(String instanceId, String region, Instant from, Instant to,
 150:             Instant now) {
 151:         if (availability.isDenied(region)) {
 152:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 153:         }
 154:         // ?뵶 ?먯옄 ?덉빟 ??"?붿뿬 ?뺤씤 ???몄텧"? ?숈떆 ?붿껌??媛숈? ?붿뿬瑜??쎄퀬 ?꾨? ?듦낵???곹븳???섎뒗??肄붾뜳??짠6).
 155:         //    ?덉빟遺?以??ㅼ젣濡????섍컙 肄쒖? finally?먯꽌 諛섎궔 ??移댁슫?곗뿏 ?ㅼ젣 諛쒖깮 ?섎쭔 ?⑤뒗??
 156:         if (!availability.reserve(CloudWatchAvailability.METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST)) {
 157:             log.warn("cloud metrics budget exhausted: used={} cap={}", availability.usedThisMonth(),
 158:                     availability.cap());
 159:             return degraded("disabled", "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
 160:         }
 161:         int period = resolvePeriodSeconds(from, to, now);
 162:         Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 163:         int calls = 0;
 164:         try {
 165:             for (MetricSpec metric : CW_METRICS) {
 166:                 calls++;
 167:                 List<Datapoint> points = aws.getMetricStatistics(region, NAMESPACE, metric.metricName(),
 168:                         "InstanceId", instanceId, from, to, period, metric.statistic());
 169:                 series.put(metric.key(), points.stream()
 170:                         // 臾몄꽌 紐낆떆: ?곗씠?고룷?명듃???쒓컙?쒖씠 ?꾨땲?????ш린???뺣젹??FE 遺?댁쓣 ?놁븻??
 171:                         .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Datapoint::timestamp))
 172:                         .map(p -> Map.of("time", p.timestamp().toString(),
 173:                                 "value", statisticValue(p, metric.statistic())))
 174:                         .toList());
 175:             }
 176:         } catch (AwsServiceException e) {
 177:             if (isAccessDenied(e)) {
 178:                 // ???곕뒗 怨좉컼? ?뺤긽 ?곹깭 ??由ъ쟾 ?⑥쐞 TTL 罹먯떆濡??ъ떆?꾨? 硫덉텛怨?議곗슜??媛뺣벑?쒕떎.
 179:                 availability.markDenied(region);
 180:                 log.info("CloudWatch access denied in {} ??metrics degraded: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 181:                 return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 182:             }
 183:             log.warn("CloudWatch metrics unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 184:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_UNAVAILABLE");
 185:         } finally {
 186:             // ?덉빟 5 - ?ㅼ젣 ?섍컙 肄?= 諛섎궔. ?ㅽ뙣??肄쒕룄 AWS ?낆옣?먯꽑 ?붿껌?대?濡??ㅼ젣 ?섎뒗 洹몃?濡??④릿??
 187:             availability.release(CloudWatchAvailability.METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST - calls);
 188:         }
 189:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 190:         collection.put("state", "ok");
 191:         collection.put("reason", null);
 192:         collection.put("periodSeconds", period);
 193:         collection.put("budget", Map.of("used", availability.usedThisMonth(), "cap", availability.cap()));
 194:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 195:         result.put("items", series);
 196:         result.put("collection", collection);
 197:         return result;
 198:     }
 199: 
 200:     /**
 201:      * Period ?곗젙 ??議고쉶 湲곌컙怨?CloudWatch retention 洹쒖튃???④퍡 留뚯”?쒗궓??
 202:      * 洹쒖튃(怨듭떇 臾몄꽌): ?쒖옉??15???댁쟾?대㈃ 300s 諛곗닔, 63???댁쟾?대㈃ 3600s 諛곗닔. ?ш린??
 203:      * "?몄텧??理쒕? 1,440 ?ъ씤??瑜?base 諛곗닔 ?뺣?濡?吏?⑤떎.
 204:      */
 205:     static int resolvePeriodSeconds(Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 206:         int base = from.isAfter(now.minus(Duration.ofDays(63))) ? 300 : 3600;
 207:         long rangeSeconds = Math.max(1, Duration.between(from, to).getSeconds());
 208:         long minPeriod = (long) Math.ceil(rangeSeconds / (double) MAX_DATAPOINTS);
 209:         long multiples = Math.max(1, (long) Math.ceil(minPeriod / (double) base));
 210:         return (int) (multiples * base);
 211:     }
 212: 
 213:     private static boolean isAccessDenied(AwsServiceException e) {
 214:         String code = e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode();
 215:         return e.statusCode() == 403
 216:                 || "AccessDenied".equals(code) || "AccessDeniedException".equals(code)
 217:                 || "UnauthorizedOperation".equals(code);
 218:     }
 219: 
 220:     private static double statisticValue(Datapoint p, Statistic statistic) {
 221:         Double value = statistic == Statistic.AVERAGE ? p.average()
 222:                 : statistic == Statistic.MAXIMUM ? p.maximum()
 223:                         : statistic == Statistic.MINIMUM ? p.minimum() : p.sum();
 224:         return value == null ? 0d : value;
 225:     }
 226: 
 227:     /** 泥?Series(?묐떟???곕뒗 寃? 湲곗? ??鍮꾩뼱 ?덉? ?딄퀬 留덉?留??ъ씤?멸? AGENT_FRESHNESS ?대궡. */
 228:     private static boolean fresh(List<Series> series, Instant now) {
 229:         long latest = latestEpoch(series);
 230:         return latest > 0 && Instant.ofEpochSecond(latest).isAfter(now.minus(AGENT_FRESHNESS));
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     private static long latestEpoch(List<Series> series) {
 234:         if (series.isEmpty()) {
 235:             return 0L;
 236:         }
 237:         return series.get(0).points().stream().mapToLong(p -> p.epoch()).max().orElse(0L);
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     private static List<Map<String, Object>> toPoints(List<Series> series) {
 241:         return series.get(0).points().stream()
 242:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparingLong(p -> p.epoch()))
 243:                 .map(p -> Map.<String, Object>of("time", Instant.ofEpochSecond(p.epoch()).toString(),
 244:                         "value", p.value()))
 245:                 .toList();
 246:     }
 247: 
 248:     private static Map<String, Object> emptySeries() {
 249:         Map<String, Object> series = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 250:         for (String key : ALL_KEYS) {
 251:             series.put(key, List.of());
 252:         }
 253:         return series;
 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     /** 媛뺣벑 ?묐떟 ??紐⑤뱺 吏???ㅻ? 鍮?諛곗뿴濡??좎???FE媛 ??議댁옱 ?щ?濡?遺꾧린?섏? ?딄쾶 ?쒕떎. */
 257:     private static Map<String, Object> degraded(String state, String reason) {
 258:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 259:         collection.put("state", state);
 260:         collection.put("reason", reason);
 261:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 262:         result.put("items", emptySeries());
 263:         result.put("collection", collection);
 264:         return result;
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     private record MetricSpec(String key, String metricName, Statistic statistic) {
 268:     }
 269: }

   1: package com.dmove.moveseye.aws;
   2: 
   3: import com.dmove.moveseye.aws.repository.CloudApiBudgetRepository;
   4: import java.time.Duration;
   5: import java.time.Instant;
   6: import java.time.LocalDate;
   7: import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
   8: import java.util.Map;
   9: import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
  10: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  11: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
  12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * CloudWatch 媛?⑹꽦 ?먯젙 ??吏??議고쉶({@link CloudMetricsService})? 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?쒖떆媛 怨듭쑀?쒕떎.
  16:  *
  17:  * <p>?뵶 ?묎렐 嫄곕? 罹먯떆??<b>由ъ쟾 ?⑥쐞</b>??肄붾뜳??寃??諛섏쁺, devdesign_ec2-monitoring-sources.md 짠8) ?? * ??由ъ쟾??沅뚰븳 ?ㅽ뙣媛 ?ㅻⅨ 由ъ쟾???뺤긽 議고쉶瑜?留됱쑝硫????쒕떎. ?ш린????由ъ뀑(?꾨줈釉?1???ы솗?몄씠 留욌떎).
  18:  */
  19: @Component
  20: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  21: public class CloudWatchAvailability {
  22: 
  23:     /** 沅뚰븳 嫄곕? ???ъ떆???듭젣 ?쒓컙 ??留?議고쉶媛 嫄곕??뱁븯硫?濡쒓렇瑜?梨꾩슦??寃껋쓣 留됰뒗?? */
  24:     private static final Duration DENIED_TTL = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  25:     /**
  26:      * ?곸꽭 吏??1??議고쉶媛 ?곕뒗 CW 肄???吏??5媛?횞 肄?1). 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?먯젙怨?`/ec2/metrics`媛
  27:      * <b>媛숈? 湲곗?</b>???⑥빞 ?쒕떎 ??紐⑸줉? cloudwatch???덈뒗???곸꽭媛 BUDGET_EXHAUSTED硫?嫄곗쭞留먯씠??肄붾뜳??짠5).
  28:      */
  29:     public static final int METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST = 5;
  30: 
  31:     private final CloudApiBudgetRepository budgetRepository;
  32: 
  33:     /** ?곕━ 紐????곹븳(D3: ?꾨━?곗뼱???덈컲). */
  34:     @Value("${app.cloud.metrics-monthly-call-cap:500000}")
  35:     private long monthlyCallCap;
  36: 
  37:     private final Map<String, Instant> deniedUntilByRegion = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  38: 
  39:     public void markDenied(String region) {
  40:         deniedUntilByRegion.put(region, Instant.now().plus(DENIED_TTL));
  41:     }
  42: 
  43:     public boolean isDenied(String region) {
  44:         Instant until = deniedUntilByRegion.get(region);
  45:         return until != null && Instant.now().isBefore(until);
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** ?대쾲 ???ъ슜??議고쉶(?덉궛 ?쒖떆쨌媛??怨듭슜). */
  49:     public long usedThisMonth() {
  50:         String month = LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM"));
  51:         return budgetRepository.findById(month).map(b -> b.getCalls()).orElse(0L);
  52:     }
  53: 
  54:     public long cap() {
  55:         return monthlyCallCap;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     /** ?덉궛 ?뚯쭊 ?щ?(?쒖떆?? ??nextCalls留뚰겮 ??遺瑜??ъ쑀媛 ?녿뒗媛. ?ㅼ젣 ?몄텧 ?꾩뿏 {@link #reserve}瑜??대떎. */
  59:     public boolean budgetExhausted(int nextCalls) {
  60:         return usedThisMonth() + nextCalls > monthlyCallCap;
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     /**
  64:      * ?몄텧 ???먯옄 ?덉빟 ???곹븳 ?덉뿉?쒕쭔 n嫄댁쓣 ?좎젏?쒕떎. false硫??몄텧?섏? ?딅뒗??
  65:      * ?숈떆 ?붿껌??媛숈? ?붿뿬瑜??쎄퀬 ?꾨? ?듦낵?섎뒗 寃쏀빀??DB 議곌굔遺 媛깆떊?쇰줈 留됰뒗??肄붾뜳??짠6).
  66:      */
  67:     public boolean reserve(int calls) {
  68:         return budgetRepository.reserve(currentMonth(), calls, monthlyCallCap) > 0;
  69:     }
  70: 
  71:     /** ?덉빟?덉쑝?????섍컙 肄?諛섎궔(?덉쇅濡?以묐떒??寃쎌슦). ?ㅼ젣 ?섍컙 ?섎쭔 ?⑤뒗?? */
  72:     public void release(int unusedCalls) {
  73:         if (unusedCalls > 0) {
  74:             budgetRepository.addCalls(currentMonth(), -unusedCalls);
  75:         }
  76:     }
  77: 
  78:     private String currentMonth() {
  79:         return LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM"));
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     /**
  83:      * 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?쒖떆???붿빟 ?먯젙: 'ok' | 'denied' | 'budget'.
  84:      * ?좑툘 AWS瑜??몄텧?섏? ?딅뒗????罹먯떆쨌移댁슫?곕쭔 蹂몃떎(?곹깭 ?먯젙 臾댄샇異??먯튃怨??숈씪).
  85:      * ?덉궛 湲곗?? ?곸꽭 議고쉶 1?뚮텇({@link #METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST})怨??숈씪?섍쾶.
  86:      */
  87:     public String availability(String region) {
  88:         if (isDenied(region)) {
  89:             return "denied";
  90:         }
  91:         if (budgetExhausted(METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST)) {
  92:             return "budget";
  93:         }
  94:         return "ok";
  95:     }
  96: }

   1: package com.dmove.moveseye.aws;

  32: /** NestJS src/aws/aws.service.ts ?ы똿 (AWS SDK v2). */
  33: @Slf4j
  34: @Service
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class AwsServiceImpl implements AwsService {
  37: 
  38:     /** 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??吏??蹂묐젹 ?몄텧 ?곹븳(由ъ쟾??留롮븘???ㅻ젅????＜ 諛⑹?). */
  39:     private static final int MAX_PARALLELISM = 16;
  40: 
  41:     private final AwsReadOnlyGateway aws;
  42:     private final CloudSnapshotQueryService snapshotQuery;
  43:     private final Ec2ZabbixMatcher matcher;
  44:     private final CloudWatchAvailability cloudWatchAvailability;
  45: 
  46:     /**
  47:      * 紐⑤땲?곕쭅 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?먯젙(?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪, devdesign_ec2-monitoring-sources.md 짠4 ??4媛?:
  48:      * zabbix-agent(留ㅼ묶+?좎꽑) / cloudwatch(CW 媛?? / unavailable(沅뚰븳쨌?덉궛쨌?섏쭛 ?ㅽ뙣) /
  49:      * unmonitored(以묒? ?????꾩옱 吏?쒓? ?대뵒?먮룄 ?놁쓬). AWS 異붽? ?몄텧 0(留ㅼ쿂 罹먯떆 + CW 媛?⑹꽦 罹먯떆留?.
  50:      */
  51:     String monitoredBy(Instance instance, String region) {
  52:         // ?뵶 stopped瑜?癒쇱? ??諛⑷툑 猿먯뼱??LAST_CHECK媛 30遺??덉씠硫?agent濡??몃뒗 ?ㅻ쪟(肄붾뜳??짠4).
  53:         String state = instance.state() == null ? "unknown" : instance.state().nameAsString();
  54:         if ("stopped".equals(state)) {
  55:             return "unmonitored";
  56:         }
  57:         java.util.Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = matcher.matchFor(instance);
  58:         if (match.isPresent() && matcher.isFresh(match.get(), java.time.Duration.ofMinutes(30))) {
  59:             return "zabbix-agent";
  60:         }
  61:         if ("ok".equals(cloudWatchAvailability.availability(region))) {
  62:             return "cloudwatch";
  63:         }
  64:         return "unavailable";
  65:     }
  66: 
  67:     /**
  68:      * ?щ윭 ?묒뾽???쒗븳???ㅻ젅?쒗??먯꽌 蹂묐젹 ?ㅽ뻾?섍퀬 ?쒖텧 ?쒖꽌?濡?寃곌낵瑜?紐⑥???
  69:      * 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??由ъ쟾??AWS ?몄텧 ?섏떗 ?????쒖감?믩퀝?щ줈 諛붽퓭 踰쎌떆怨??쒓컙???⑷퀎?먯꽌 理쒕? 1嫄댁쑝濡??⑥텞.
  70:      * 媛쒕퀎 ?묒뾽 ?ㅽ뙣??null濡??섏쭛(?몄텧遺?먯꽌 skip). ?묒뾽 肄쒕갚? ?먯껜?곸쑝濡??덉쇅瑜?泥섎━??遺遺꾧컪??諛섑솚?섎뒗 寃껋쓣 沅뚯옣.
  71:      */
  72:     private <T> List<T> runParallel(List<? extends Callable<T>> tasks) {
  73:         if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
  74:             return List.of();
  75:         }
  76:         if (tasks.size() == 1) {
  77:             try {
  78:                 return new ArrayList<>(List.of(tasks.get(0).call()));
  79:             } catch (Exception e) {
  80:                 log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  81:                 List<T> single = new ArrayList<>();
  82:                 single.add(null);
  83:                 return single;
  84:             }
  85:         }
  86:         ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Math.min(tasks.size(), MAX_PARALLELISM));
  87:         try {
  88:             List<Future<T>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
  89:             for (Callable<T> task : tasks) {
  90:                 futures.add(pool.submit(task));
  91:             }
  92:             List<T> results = new ArrayList<>();
  93:             for (Future<T> f : futures) {
  94:                 try {
  95:                     results.add(f.get());
  96:                 } catch (Exception e) {
  97:                     log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  98:                     results.add(null);
  99:                 }
 100:             }
 101:             return results;
 102:         } finally {
 103:             pool.shutdown();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:

 108:     public List<String> getRegions() {
 109:         // opted-in 由ъ쟾???ы븿 ??湲곗〈 "opt-in-not-required留?? ?ъ슜?먭? 耳?由ъ쟾??紐⑸줉쨌媛쒖슂?먯꽌 鍮좊쑉?몃떎
 110:         // (肄붾뜳??寃??諛쒓껄). ?ㅻ깄?력룹텛?뺢낵 媛숈? 湲곗?(enabledRegionNames)?쇰줈 ?듭씪.
 111:         return aws.enabledRegionNames();
 112:     }
 113: 
 114:     @Override
 115:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcs(String keyword, List<String> regions) {
 116:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 117:         List<Callable<List<Map<String, Object>>>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 118:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 119:             tasks.add(() -> vpcsForRegion(region, keyword));
 120:         }
 121:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 122:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 123:             if (part != null) {
 124:                 vpcs.addAll(part);
 125:             }
 126:         }
 127:         return Map.of("items", vpcs);
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??VPC 紐⑸줉. AWS ?몄텧 ?ㅽ뙣??濡쒓렇 ??鍮?紐⑸줉(?먮낯??region 猷⑦봽 ??skip 嫄곕룞 ?좎?). */
 131:     private List<Map<String, Object>> vpcsForRegion(String region, String keyword) {
 132:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 133:         try {
 134:             List<Vpc> vpcList = aws.describeVpcs(region);
 135:             if (vpcList.isEmpty()) {
 136:                 return vpcs;
 137:             }
 138:             List<Subnet> subnets = aws.describeSubnets(region);
 139:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 140: 
 141:             // vpcId ??subnet??mapPublicIpOnLaunch 紐⑸줉
 142:             Map<String, List<Boolean>> subnetMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 143:             for (Subnet s : subnets) {
 144:                 if (s.vpcId() != null) {
 145:                     subnetMap.computeIfAbsent(s.vpcId(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 146:                             .add(Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.mapPublicIpOnLaunch()));
 147:                 }
 148:             }
 149:             // vpcId ??instance ??
 150:             Map<String, Integer> instanceCount = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 151:             for (Instance i : instances) {
 152:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 153:                     instanceCount.merge(i.vpcId(), 1, Integer::sum);
 154:                 }
 155:             }
 156: 
 157:             for (Vpc vpc : vpcList) {
 158:                 String vpcName = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 159:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 160:                 if (keyword != null && !vpcName.toLowerCase().contains(keyword.toLowerCase())) {
 161:                     continue;
 162:                 }
 163:                 List<Boolean> subList = subnetMap.get(vpc.vpcId());
 164:                 String blockPublicAccess = (subList != null && subList.stream().allMatch(b -> !b)) ? "Off" : "On";
 165: 
 166:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 167:                 item.put("vpcId", vpc.vpcId());
 168:                 item.put("name", vpcName);
 169:                 item.put("ownerId", vpc.ownerId());
 170:                 item.put("region", region);
 171:                 item.put("state", vpc.stateAsString());
 172:                 item.put("ec2Count", instanceCount.getOrDefault(vpc.vpcId(), 0));
 173:                 item.put("ipv4Cidr", vpc.cidrBlock());
 174:                 item.put("ipv6Cidr", vpc.ipv6CidrBlockAssociationSet().stream()
 175:                         .map(x -> x.ipv6CidrBlock()).toList());
 176:                 item.put("tenancy", vpc.instanceTenancyAsString());
 177:                 item.put("blockPublicAccess", blockPublicAccess);
 178:                 vpcs.add(item);
 179:             }
 180:         } catch (Exception e) {
 181:             log.error("Error get VPC on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 182:         }
 183:         return vpcs;
 184:     }

 202:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??EC2 ?곸꽭 紐⑸줉. */
 203:     private List<Map<String, Object>> instancesForRegion(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 204:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 205:         try {
 206:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 207:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 208:                 return ec2s;
 209:             }
 210:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 211:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 212:             Set<String> vpcIdSet = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 213:             instances.forEach(i -> {
 214:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 215:                     vpcIdSet.add(i.vpcId());
 216:                 }
 217:             });
 218: 
 219:             Map<String, String> statusMap = instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 220:             Map<String, String> vpcNameMap = vpcNameMap(region, new ArrayList<>(vpcIdSet));
 221: 
 222:             for (Instance inst : instances) {
 223:                 if (vpcIds != null && (inst.vpcId() == null || !vpcIds.contains(inst.vpcId()))) {
 224:                     continue;
 225:                 }
 226:                 String vpcId = inst.vpcId() != null ? inst.vpcId() : "-";
 227:                 String name = inst.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 228:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 229: 
 230:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 231:                 item.put("instanceName", name);
 232:                 item.put("instanceId", inst.instanceId());
 233:                 item.put("instanceState", inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : "-");
 234:                 item.put("instanceType", inst.instanceTypeAsString() != null ? inst.instanceTypeAsString() : "-");
 235:                 item.put("statusCheck", statusMap.getOrDefault(inst.instanceId(), "-"));
 236:                 item.put("region", region);
 237:                 item.put("availabilityZone", inst.placement() != null ? inst.placement().availabilityZone() : "-");
 238:                 item.put("vpcName", vpcNameMap.getOrDefault(vpcId, vpcId));
 239:                 item.put("vpcId", vpcId);
 240:                 item.put("securityGroupName", inst.securityGroups().stream()
 241:                         .map(g -> g.groupName()).reduce((a, b) -> a + ", " + b).orElse("-"));
 242:                 // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?????몄뒪?댁뒪??硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕媛 ?녿뒗媛"瑜?紐⑸줉???듯븳??
 243:                 item.put("monitoredBy", monitoredBy(inst, region));
 244:                 ec2s.add(item);
 245:             }
 246:         } catch (Exception e) {
 247:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 248:         }
 249:         return ec2s;
 250:     }

 269:     private RegionInstances instancesWithStatus(String region) {
 270:         try {
 271:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 272:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 273:                 return null;
 274:             }
 275:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 276:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 277:             return new RegionInstances(region, instances, instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds));

 284:     @Override
 285:     public Map<String, Object> summaryInstances(List<String> regions) {
 286:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 287:         List<Callable<RegionInstances>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 288:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 289:             tasks.add(() -> instancesWithStatus(region));
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByRegion = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 293:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 294:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByState = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 295:         Map<String, Map<String, Integer>> ec2ByRegionAndStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 296: 
 297:         // AWS ?몄텧? 蹂묐젹, 吏묎퀎???쒖꽌 蹂댁〈 ?⑥씪 ?ㅻ젅??硫깅벑)濡??섑뻾.
 298:         for (RegionInstances ri : runParallel(tasks)) {
 299:             if (ri == null) {
 300:                 continue;
 301:             }
 302:             ec2ByRegion.merge(ri.region(), ri.instances().size(), Integer::sum);
 303:             ec2ByRegionAndStatus.put(ri.region(), new LinkedHashMap<>());
 304:             for (Instance inst : ri.instances()) {
 305:                 ec2ByType.merge(inst.instanceTypeAsString(), 1, Integer::sum);
 306:                 ec2ByState.merge(inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : null, 1, Integer::sum);
 307:                 String status = ri.statusMap().get(inst.instanceId());
 308:                 ec2ByRegionAndStatus.get(ri.region()).merge(status, 1, Integer::sum);
 309:             }
 310:         }
 311: 
 312:         Map<String, Object> items = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 313:         items.put("ec2ByRegion", ec2ByRegion);
 314:         items.put("ec2ByType", ec2ByType);
 315:         items.put("ec2ByState", ec2ByState);
 316:         items.put("ec2ByRegionAndStatus", ec2ByRegionAndStatus);
 317:         return Map.of("items", items);
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     /** 臾닿낵湲?媛쒖슂: EC2 ?몃깽?좊━? ?곹깭 ?먭?留??ъ슜?쒕떎. */
 321:     @Override
 322:     public Map<String, Object> overview(List<String> regions, TimeRange timeRange) {
 323:         List<String> targets = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 324:         List<Callable<OverviewRegion>> tasks = targets.stream()
 325:                 .map(region -> (Callable<OverviewRegion>) () -> overviewForRegion(region)).toList();
 326:         List<OverviewRegion> parts = runParallel(tasks);
 327:         List<Map<String, Object>> collection = new ArrayList<>();
 328:         List<Map<String, Object>> risks = new ArrayList<>();
 329:         int healthy=0, warning=0, critical=0, unobserved=0, stopped=0, total=0;
 330:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 331:             if (p == null) continue;
 332:             collection.add(p.collection()); risks.addAll(p.risks());

 340:         }

 342:         risks.forEach(r -> r.remove("rank"));

 361:     private OverviewRegion overviewForRegion(String region) {
 362:         Instant collected = Instant.now();
 363:         List<Instance> instances;
 364:         Map<String,String> statuses;
 365:         try {
 366:             instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 367:             statuses = instanceStatuses(region, instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId).toList());
 368:         } catch (Exception e) {
 369:             return OverviewRegion.unavailable(region, "EC2_UNAVAILABLE");
 370:         }
 371:         List<Map<String,Object>> risks=new ArrayList<>();
 372:         Map<String,Integer> coverage=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 373:         int healthy=0,warning=0,critical=0,unobserved=0,stopped=0;
 374:         for (Instance i : instances) {
 375:             coverage.merge(monitoredBy(i, region), 1, Integer::sum);
 376:             String state=i.state()==null ? "unknown" : i.state().nameAsString(); String check=statuses.get(i.instanceId());
 377:             // ?먯젙 洹쒖튃? ?ㅻ깄???섏쭛(CloudSnapshotService)怨?怨듭쑀?쒕떎 ??蹂듭젣 湲덉?.
 378:             String severity = Ec2StatusRules.classify(state, check);
 379:             switch (severity) {
 380:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_STOPPED -> stopped++;
 381:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_CRITICAL -> critical++;
 382:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_WARNING -> warning++;
 383:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_UNOBSERVED -> unobserved++;
 384:                 default -> healthy++;
 385:             }
 386:             if (!"healthy".equals(severity)) { Map<String,Object> x=overviewInstance(i,region,check,severity); x.put("rank", "critical".equals(severity)?4:"warning".equals(severity)?3:"unobserved".equals(severity)?2:1); risks.add(x); }
 387:         }
 388:         Map<String,Object> ec2 = collectionState("ok", collected.toString(), null);
 389:         Map<String,Object> disabled = collectionState("disabled", null, "ZERO_COST_MODE");
 390:         return new OverviewRegion(Map.of("region",region,"ec2",ec2,"cloudWatch",disabled,"alarms",disabled), instances.size(),healthy,warning,critical,unobserved,stopped,risks,coverage);
 391:     }

 491:     /**
 492:      * AWS SDK??resource-specific not-found留??덉젙??DMOVE API ?ㅻ쪟濡?諛붽씔??
 493:      *
 494:      * <p>{@code .Malformed}??not-found濡?痍④툒?쒕떎 ??AWS???뺤떇???닿툔??ID??NotFound ???
 495:      * Malformed瑜??섏??붾뜲(?고????ㅼ륫 2026-08-24: {@code Invalid id} ??500 ?좎텧), ?ъ슜??
 496:      * ?낆옣?먯꽑 ????"洹몃윴 由ъ냼???놁쓬"?대떎. 援щ텇??遊ㅼ옄 ?덈궡媛 ?щ씪吏吏 ?딅뒗??
 497:      */
 498:     static ErrorCode awsNotFoundError(String resource, String awsErrorCode) {
 499:         if (awsErrorCode == null || !(awsErrorCode.endsWith(".NotFound") || awsErrorCode.endsWith(".Malformed"))) {
 500:             return null;
 501:         }
 502:         return "instance".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Instance")
 503:                 ? ErrorCode.INSTANCE_NOT_FOUND
 504:                 : "vpc".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Vpc")
 505:                         ? ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND : null;
 506:     }

 513:                     end.minus(java.time.Duration.ofDays(90)), end).stream().map(event -> {

 515:                 x.put("eventTime", event.eventTime() == null ? null : event.eventTime().toString()); x.put("username", event.username());
 516:                 x.put("resources", event.resources().stream().map(resource -> Map.of("type", resource.resourceType(), "name", resource.resourceName())).toList());
 517:                 x.put("readOnly", event.readOnly()); x.put("source", event.eventSource()); return x;
 518:             }).toList();
 519:             return Map.of("items", items, "collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region,
 520:                     "retentionDays", 90, "nextTokenAvailable", false));

 539:         x.put("system", status == null || status.systemStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.systemStatus().statusAsString());
 540:         x.put("instance", status == null || status.instanceStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.instanceStatus().statusAsString()); return x;
 541:     }

 563:     private Map<String, String> instanceStatuses(String region, List<String> instanceIds) {
 564:         Map<String, String> statusMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 565:         if (instanceIds.isEmpty()) {
 566:             return statusMap;
 567:         }
 568:         List<InstanceStatus> statuses = aws.describeInstanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 569:         for (InstanceStatus s : statuses) {
 570:             int passed = 0;
 571:             if (s.systemStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.systemStatus().statusAsString())) {
 572:                 passed++;
 573:             }
 574:             if (s.instanceStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.instanceStatus().statusAsString())) {
 575:                 passed++;
 576:             }
 577:             String check = passed == 2 ? "2/2 checks passed" : passed == 1 ? "1/2 checks failed" : "0/2 checks failed";
 578:             statusMap.put(s.instanceId(), check);
 579:         }
 580:         return statusMap;
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     private Map<String, String> vpcNameMap(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 584:         Map<String, String> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 585:         if (vpcIds.isEmpty()) {
 586:             return map;
 587:         }
 588:         for (Vpc vpc : aws.describeVpcs(region, vpcIds)) {
 589:             String name = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 590:                     .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse(vpc.vpcId());
 591:             map.put(vpc.vpcId(), name);
 592:         }
 593:         return map;
 594:     }

 596:     private String str(Object o) {
 597:         return o != null ? o.toString() : null;
 598:     }

  81:     @Override
  82:     public List<InventoryHost> listHosts(SourceRef src, CallerContext ctx, HostQuery query) {
  83:         String token = sessionProvider.resolve(ctx);
  84:         try {
  85:             Map<String, Object> params = new LinkedHashMap<>();
  86:             params.put("output", HOST_OUTPUT);
  87:             params.put("selectInterfaces", INTERFACE_FIELDS);
  88:             if (query.entities() != null && !query.entities().isEmpty()) {
  89:                 params.put("hostids", query.entities().stream().map(EntityRef::nativeId).toList());
  90:             }
  91:             if (query.groupIds() != null && !query.groupIds().isEmpty()) {
  92:                 params.put("groupids", query.groupIds());
  93:             }
  94:             // ?좏깮 ??ぉ(짠HostInclude) ??鍮꾩슜 ?덈뒗 ?쒕툕議고쉶???붿껌?덉쓣 ?뚮쭔 嫄대떎.
  95:             if (query.wants(HostInclude.GROUPS)) {
  96:                 params.put("selectGroups", GROUP_FIELDS);
  97:             }
  98:             if (query.wants(HostInclude.LAST_CHECK)) {
  99:                 params.put("selectItems", ITEM_LASTCLOCK_FIELDS);
 100:             }
 101:             if (query.wants(HostInclude.TAGS)) {
 102:                 params.put("selectTags", TAGS_EXTEND);
 103:             }
 104:             if (query.wants(HostInclude.OS)) {
 105:                 params.put("selectInventory", INVENTORY_OS_FIELDS);
 106:             }
 107: 
 108:             List<Map<String, Object>> hosts = hostService.getRaw(token, params);
 109:             List<InventoryHost> out = new ArrayList<>(hosts.size());
 110:             for (Map<String, Object> host : hosts) {
 111:                 out.add(toInventoryHost(host));
 112:             }
 113:             return out;
 114:         } catch (RuntimeException e) {
 115:             throw translate(e);
 116:         }
 117:     }

 119:     @Override
 120:     public List<InventoryGroup> listGroups(SourceRef src, CallerContext ctx, GroupQuery query) {
 121:         String token = sessionProvider.resolve(ctx);
 122:         try {
 123:             Map<String, Object> params = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 124:             if (query.groupIds() != null && !query.groupIds().isEmpty()) {
 125:                 params.put("groupids", query.groupIds());
 126:             }
 127:             if (query.includeHosts()) {
 128:                 params.put("selectHosts", GROUP_MEMBER_FIELDS);
 129:             }
 130: 
 131:             List<Map<String, Object>> groups = hostService.getHostGroupsRaw(token, params);
 132:             List<InventoryGroup> out = new ArrayList<>(groups.size());
 133:             for (Map<String, Object> group : groups) {
 134:                 out.add(new InventoryGroup(
 135:                         EntityRef.ofDefault(str(group.get("groupid"))),
 136:                         str(group.get("name")),
 137:                         toHostRefs(group.get("hosts"))));
 138:             }
 139:             return out;
 140:         } catch (RuntimeException e) {
 141:             throw translate(e);
 142:         }

 146:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 147:     private static List<NamedEntity> toHostRefs(Object rawHosts) {
 148:         if (!(rawHosts instanceof List)) {
 149:             return List.of();
 150:         }
 151:         List<NamedEntity> out = new ArrayList<>();
 152:         for (Object o : (List<Object>) rawHosts) {
 153:             if (o instanceof Map<?, ?> h) {
 154:                 String id = str(h.get("hostid"));
 155:                 if (id != null) {
 156:                     out.add(new NamedEntity(EntityRef.ofDefault(id), str(h.get("name"))));
 157:                 }
 158:             }
 159:         }
 160:         return out;
 161:     }

 163:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 164:     private InventoryHost toInventoryHost(Map<String, Object> host) {
 165:         EntityRef ref = EntityRef.ofDefault(str(host.get("hostid")));
 166:         String name = str(host.get("name"));
 167:         List<Map<String, Object>> rawInterfaces = host.get("interfaces") instanceof List
 168:                 ? (List<Map<String, Object>>) host.get("interfaces") : List.of();
 169:         List<HostNetworkInterface> interfaces = new ArrayList<>(rawInterfaces.size());
 170:         for (Map<String, Object> iface : rawInterfaces) {
 171:             interfaces.add(new HostNetworkInterface(
 172:                     interfaceKind(str(iface.get("type"))),
 173:                     availability(str(iface.get("available"))),
 174:                     str(iface.get("ip")),
 175:                     MAIN_PRIMARY.equals(str(iface.get("main"))),
 176:                     str(iface.get("interfaceid")),
 177:                     // useip: "1"=ip ?묒냽, "0"=DNS ?묒냽. ?놁쑝硫?誘몄긽(null) ??留ㅼ쿂媛 DNS ?몄뒪?몃? IP 洹쇨굅?먯꽌 類??
 178:                     iface.get("useip") == null ? null : "1".equals(str(iface.get("useip")))));
 179:         }
 180:         String status = str(host.get("status"));
 181:         return new InventoryHost(ref, name, interfaces,
 182:                 toGroups(host.get("groups")), toLastCheckEpoch(host.get("items")),
 183:                 toTags(host.get("tags")),
 184:                 status == null ? null : HOST_STATUS_MONITORED.equals(status),
 185:                 toOsName(host.get("inventory")));
 186:     }

 189:      * selectInventory 寃곌낵?먯꽌 OS留?戮묐뒗??4c-4f). 誘몄슂泥?遺?щ㈃ null.

 191:      * <p>?좑툘 Zabbix???몃깽?좊━ 紐⑤뱶媛 爰쇱쭊 ?몄뒪?몄뿉 {@code inventory}瑜?<b>鍮?諛곗뿴</b>濡?以??Map???꾨땲??.

 194:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 195:     private static String toOsName(Object rawInventory) {
 196:         if (!(rawInventory instanceof Map)) {
 197:             return null;
 198:         }
 199:         String os = str(((Map<String, Object>) rawInventory).get("os"));
 200:         return os == null || os.isBlank() ? null : os;
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     /** selectTags 寃곌낵 ??以묐┰ ?쒓렇. 誘몄슂泥?遺?щ㈃ 鍮?由ъ뒪?? */
 204:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 205:     private static List<HostTag> toTags(Object rawTags) {
 206:         if (!(rawTags instanceof List)) {
 207:             return List.of();
 208:         }
 209:         List<HostTag> out = new ArrayList<>();
 210:         for (Object o : (List<Object>) rawTags) {
 211:             if (o instanceof Map<?, ?> t) {
 212:                 out.add(new HostTag(str(t.get("tag")), str(t.get("value"))));
 213:             }
 214:         }
 215:         return out;
 216:     }
 217: 
 218:     /** selectGroups 寃곌낵 ??以묐┰ 洹몃９ 李몄“. 誘몄슂泥?遺?щ㈃ 鍮?由ъ뒪?? */
 219:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 220:     private static List<HostGroupRef> toGroups(Object rawGroups) {
 221:         if (!(rawGroups instanceof List)) {
 222:             return List.of();
 223:         }
 224:         List<HostGroupRef> out = new ArrayList<>();
 225:         for (Object o : (List<Object>) rawGroups) {
 226:             if (o instanceof Map<?, ?> g) {
 227:                 out.add(new HostGroupRef(str(g.get("groupid")), str(g.get("name"))));
 228:             }
 229:         }
 230:         return out;
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     /**
 234:      * selectItems 寃곌낵 ??留덉?留??섏쭛 ?쒓컖(?꾩씠??{@code lastclock} 理쒕?媛? epoch 珥?.
 235:      * 誘몄슂泥?遺???꾨? 0?대㈃ null(= ?섏쭛 ?대젰 ?놁쓬).
 236:      */
 237:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
 238:     private static Long toLastCheckEpoch(Object rawItems) {
 239:         if (!(rawItems instanceof List)) {
 240:             return null;
 241:         }
 242:         long max = 0;
 243:         for (Object o : (List<Object>) rawItems) {
 244:             if (o instanceof Map<?, ?> item) {
 245:                 Object lastclock = item.get("lastclock");
 246:                 if (lastclock != null) {
 247:                     try {
 248:                         max = Math.max(max, Long.parseLong(lastclock.toString()));
 249:                     } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
 250:                         // 鍮꾩젙??lastclock 臾댁떆(?먮낯怨??숈씪)
 251:                     }
 252:                 }
 253:             }
 254:         }
 255:         return max > 0 ? max : null;
 256:     }
 257: 
 258:     private static InterfaceKind interfaceKind(String type) {
 259:         if (type == null) {
 260:             return InterfaceKind.OTHER;
 261:         }
 262:         return switch (type) {
 263:             case TYPE_AGENT -> InterfaceKind.AGENT;
 264:             case TYPE_SNMP -> InterfaceKind.SNMP;
 265:             case TYPE_IPMI -> InterfaceKind.IPMI;
 266:             case TYPE_JMX -> InterfaceKind.JMX;
 267:             default -> InterfaceKind.OTHER;
 268:         };
 269:     }
 270: 
 271:     private static Availability availability(String available) {
 272:         if (AVAILABLE_OK.equals(available)) {
 273:             return Availability.AVAILABLE;
 274:         }
 275:         if (AVAILABLE_NO.equals(available)) {
 276:             return Availability.UNAVAILABLE;
 277:         }
 278:         return Availability.UNKNOWN;
 279:     }
 280: 
 281:     private static String str(Object o) {
 282:         return o != null ? o.toString() : null;
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     /** ?덇굅???덉쇅 ??EngineException 踰덉뿭(MetricAdapter? ?숈씪 留ㅽ븨, 짠D8). */
 286:     private EngineException translate(RuntimeException e) {
 287:         if (e instanceof EngineException ee) {
 288:             return ee;
 289:         }
 290:         if (e instanceof UnauthorizedError) {
 291:             return new EngineException(ErrorKind.UNAUTHORIZED, SourceRef.DEFAULT_SOURCE_ID, e.getMessage(), false, e);
 292:         }
 293:         if (e instanceof ApiError || e instanceof HttpError) {
 294:             return new EngineException(ErrorKind.SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, SourceRef.DEFAULT_SOURCE_ID, e.getMessage(), true, e);
 295:         }
 296:         return new EngineException(ErrorKind.INTERNAL, SourceRef.DEFAULT_SOURCE_ID, e.getMessage(), false, e);
 297:     }

 122:             //    ?듦낵??stale CPU瑜?agent ?뚯뒪濡??대낫?몃떎(肄붾뜳??寃??짠3). ?먯젙 ???= ?묐떟 ???泥?Series).
 123:             if (!fresh(cpu, now) || !fresh(memory, now)) {
 124:                 log.debug("agent series quality gate failed for host {}", match.hostid());
 125:                 return null;
 126:             }
 127:             long latestEpoch = Math.min(latestEpoch(cpu), latestEpoch(memory));
 128: 
 129:             Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 130:             series.put("cpu", toPoints(cpu));
 131:             series.put("memoryUsage", toPoints(memory));
 132:             Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 133:             collection.put("state", "ok");
 134:             collection.put("reason", null);
 135:             collection.put("source", "zabbix-agent");
 136:             collection.put("fallbackUsed", false);
 137:             collection.put("matchBasis", match.basis());
 138:             collection.put("zabbixHostid", match.hostid());
 139:             collection.put("zabbixHostName", match.hostName());

 144:             log.warn("agent series fetch failed for host {}: {}", match.hostid(), e.getMessage());
 145:             return null;
 146:         }
 147:     }
 148: 
 149:     private Map<String, Object> cloudWatchSeries(String instanceId, String region, Instant from, Instant to,
 150:             Instant now) {
 151:         if (availability.isDenied(region)) {
 152:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 153:         }
 154:         // ?뵶 ?먯옄 ?덉빟 ??"?붿뿬 ?뺤씤 ???몄텧"? ?숈떆 ?붿껌??媛숈? ?붿뿬瑜??쎄퀬 ?꾨? ?듦낵???곹븳???섎뒗??肄붾뜳??짠6).
 155:         //    ?덉빟遺?以??ㅼ젣濡????섍컙 肄쒖? finally?먯꽌 諛섎궔 ??移댁슫?곗뿏 ?ㅼ젣 諛쒖깮 ?섎쭔 ?⑤뒗??
 156:         if (!availability.reserve(CloudWatchAvailability.METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST)) {
 157:             log.warn("cloud metrics budget exhausted: used={} cap={}", availability.usedThisMonth(),
 158:                     availability.cap());
 159:             return degraded("disabled", "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
 160:         }
 161:         int period = resolvePeriodSeconds(from, to, now);
 162:         Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 163:         int calls = 0;
 164:         try {
 165:             for (MetricSpec metric : CW_METRICS) {
 166:                 calls++;
 167:                 List<Datapoint> points = aws.getMetricStatistics(region, NAMESPACE, metric.metricName(),
 168:                         "InstanceId", instanceId, from, to, period, metric.statistic());
 169:                 series.put(metric.key(), points.stream()
 170:                         // 臾몄꽌 紐낆떆: ?곗씠?고룷?명듃???쒓컙?쒖씠 ?꾨땲?????ш린???뺣젹??FE 遺?댁쓣 ?놁븻??
 171:                         .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Datapoint::timestamp))
 172:                         .map(p -> Map.of("time", p.timestamp().toString(),
 173:                                 "value", statisticValue(p, metric.statistic())))
 174:                         .toList());
 175:             }
 176:         } catch (AwsServiceException e) {
 177:             if (isAccessDenied(e)) {
 178:                 // ???곕뒗 怨좉컼? ?뺤긽 ?곹깭 ??由ъ쟾 ?⑥쐞 TTL 罹먯떆濡??ъ떆?꾨? 硫덉텛怨?議곗슜??媛뺣벑?쒕떎.
 179:                 availability.markDenied(region);
 180:                 log.info("CloudWatch access denied in {} ??metrics degraded: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 181:                 return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 182:             }
 183:             log.warn("CloudWatch metrics unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 184:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_UNAVAILABLE");
 185:         } finally {
 186:             // ?덉빟 5 - ?ㅼ젣 ?섍컙 肄?= 諛섎궔. ?ㅽ뙣??肄쒕룄 AWS ?낆옣?먯꽑 ?붿껌?대?濡??ㅼ젣 ?섎뒗 洹몃?濡??④릿??
 187:             availability.release(CloudWatchAvailability.METRICS_CALLS_PER_REQUEST - calls);
 188:         }
 189:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 190:         collection.put("state", "ok");
 191:         collection.put("reason", null);
 192:         collection.put("periodSeconds", period);
 193:         collection.put("budget", Map.of("used", availability.usedThisMonth(), "cap", availability.cap()));
 194:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 195:         result.put("items", series);
 196:         result.put("collection", collection);
 197:         return result;
 198:     }
 199: 
 200:     /**
 201:      * Period ?곗젙 ??議고쉶 湲곌컙怨?CloudWatch retention 洹쒖튃???④퍡 留뚯”?쒗궓??
 202:      * 洹쒖튃(怨듭떇 臾몄꽌): ?쒖옉??15???댁쟾?대㈃ 300s 諛곗닔, 63???댁쟾?대㈃ 3600s 諛곗닔. ?ш린??
 203:      * "?몄텧??理쒕? 1,440 ?ъ씤??瑜?base 諛곗닔 ?뺣?濡?吏?⑤떎.
 204:      */
 205:     static int resolvePeriodSeconds(Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 206:         int base = from.isAfter(now.minus(Duration.ofDays(63))) ? 300 : 3600;
 207:         long rangeSeconds = Math.max(1, Duration.between(from, to).getSeconds());
 208:         long minPeriod = (long) Math.ceil(rangeSeconds / (double) MAX_DATAPOINTS);
 209:         long multiples = Math.max(1, (long) Math.ceil(minPeriod / (double) base));
 210:         return (int) (multiples * base);
 211:     }
 212: 
 213:     private static boolean isAccessDenied(AwsServiceException e) {
 214:         String code = e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode();
 215:         return e.statusCode() == 403
 216:                 || "AccessDenied".equals(code) || "AccessDeniedException".equals(code)
 217:                 || "UnauthorizedOperation".equals(code);
 218:     }
 219: 
 220:     private static double statisticValue(Datapoint p, Statistic statistic) {
 221:         Double value = statistic == Statistic.AVERAGE ? p.average()
 222:                 : statistic == Statistic.MAXIMUM ? p.maximum()
 223:                         : statistic == Statistic.MINIMUM ? p.minimum() : p.sum();
 224:         return value == null ? 0d : value;
 225:     }
 226: 
 227:     /** 泥?Series(?묐떟???곕뒗 寃? 湲곗? ??鍮꾩뼱 ?덉? ?딄퀬 留덉?留??ъ씤?멸? AGENT_FRESHNESS ?대궡. */
 228:     private static boolean fresh(List<Series> series, Instant now) {
 229:         long latest = latestEpoch(series);
 230:         return latest > 0 && Instant.ofEpochSecond(latest).isAfter(now.minus(AGENT_FRESHNESS));
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     private static long latestEpoch(List<Series> series) {
 234:         if (series.isEmpty()) {
 235:             return 0L;
 236:         }
 237:         return series.get(0).points().stream().mapToLong(p -> p.epoch()).max().orElse(0L);
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     private static List<Map<String, Object>> toPoints(List<Series> series) {
 241:         return series.get(0).points().stream()
 242:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparingLong(p -> p.epoch()))
 243:                 .map(p -> Map.<String, Object>of("time", Instant.ofEpochSecond(p.epoch()).toString(),
 244:                         "value", p.value()))
 245:                 .toList();
 246:     }
 247: 
 248:     private static Map<String, Object> emptySeries() {
 249:         Map<String, Object> series = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 250:         for (String key : ALL_KEYS) {
 251:             series.put(key, List.of());
 252:         }
 253:         return series;
 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     /** 媛뺣벑 ?묐떟 ??紐⑤뱺 吏???ㅻ? 鍮?諛곗뿴濡??좎???FE媛 ??議댁옱 ?щ?濡?遺꾧린?섏? ?딄쾶 ?쒕떎. */
 257:     private static Map<String, Object> degraded(String state, String reason) {
 258:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 259:         collection.put("state", state);
 260:         collection.put("reason", reason);
 261:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 262:         result.put("items", emptySeries());
 263:         result.put("collection", collection);
 264:         return result;
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     private record MetricSpec(String key, String metricName, Statistic statistic) {
 268:     }
 269: }

  57:         java.util.Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = matcher.matchFor(instance);
  58:         if (match.isPresent() && matcher.isFresh(match.get(), java.time.Duration.ofMinutes(30))) {
  59:             return "zabbix-agent";
  60:         }
  61:         if ("ok".equals(cloudWatchAvailability.availability(region))) {
  62:             return "cloudwatch";
  63:         }
  64:         return "unavailable";
  65:     }
  66: 
  67:     /**
  68:      * ?щ윭 ?묒뾽???쒗븳???ㅻ젅?쒗??먯꽌 蹂묐젹 ?ㅽ뻾?섍퀬 ?쒖텧 ?쒖꽌?濡?寃곌낵瑜?紐⑥???
  69:      * 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??由ъ쟾??AWS ?몄텧 ?섏떗 ?????쒖감?믩퀝?щ줈 諛붽퓭 踰쎌떆怨??쒓컙???⑷퀎?먯꽌 理쒕? 1嫄댁쑝濡??⑥텞.
  70:      * 媛쒕퀎 ?묒뾽 ?ㅽ뙣??null濡??섏쭛(?몄텧遺?먯꽌 skip). ?묒뾽 肄쒕갚? ?먯껜?곸쑝濡??덉쇅瑜?泥섎━??遺遺꾧컪??諛섑솚?섎뒗 寃껋쓣 沅뚯옣.
  71:      */
  72:     private <T> List<T> runParallel(List<? extends Callable<T>> tasks) {
  73:         if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
  74:             return List.of();
  75:         }
  76:         if (tasks.size() == 1) {
  77:             try {
  78:                 return new ArrayList<>(List.of(tasks.get(0).call()));
  79:             } catch (Exception e) {
  80:                 log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  81:                 List<T> single = new ArrayList<>();
  82:                 single.add(null);
  83:                 return single;
  84:             }
  85:         }
  86:         ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Math.min(tasks.size(), MAX_PARALLELISM));
  87:         try {
  88:             List<Future<T>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
  89:             for (Callable<T> task : tasks) {
  90:                 futures.add(pool.submit(task));
  91:             }
  92:             List<T> results = new ArrayList<>();
  93:             for (Future<T> f : futures) {
  94:                 try {
  95:                     results.add(f.get());
  96:                 } catch (Exception e) {
  97:                     log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  98:                     results.add(null);
  99:                 }
 100:             }
 101:             return results;
 102:         } finally {
 103:             pool.shutdown();
 104:         }
 105:     }

 111:         return aws.enabledRegionNames();
 112:     }

 114:     @Override
 115:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcs(String keyword, List<String> regions) {
 116:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 117:         List<Callable<List<Map<String, Object>>>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 118:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 119:             tasks.add(() -> vpcsForRegion(region, keyword));
 120:         }
 121:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 122:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 123:             if (part != null) {
 124:                 vpcs.addAll(part);
 125:             }
 126:         }
 127:         return Map.of("items", vpcs);
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??VPC 紐⑸줉. AWS ?몄텧 ?ㅽ뙣??濡쒓렇 ??鍮?紐⑸줉(?먮낯??region 猷⑦봽 ??skip 嫄곕룞 ?좎?). */
 131:     private List<Map<String, Object>> vpcsForRegion(String region, String keyword) {
 132:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 133:         try {
 134:             List<Vpc> vpcList = aws.describeVpcs(region);
 135:             if (vpcList.isEmpty()) {
 136:                 return vpcs;
 137:             }
 138:             List<Subnet> subnets = aws.describeSubnets(region);
 139:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 140: 
 141:             // vpcId ??subnet??mapPublicIpOnLaunch 紐⑸줉
 142:             Map<String, List<Boolean>> subnetMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 143:             for (Subnet s : subnets) {
 144:                 if (s.vpcId() != null) {
 145:                     subnetMap.computeIfAbsent(s.vpcId(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 146:                             .add(Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.mapPublicIpOnLaunch()));
 147:                 }
 148:             }
 149:             // vpcId ??instance ??
 150:             Map<String, Integer> instanceCount = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 151:             for (Instance i : instances) {
 152:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 153:                     instanceCount.merge(i.vpcId(), 1, Integer::sum);
 154:                 }
 155:             }
 156: 
 157:             for (Vpc vpc : vpcList) {
 158:                 String vpcName = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 159:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 160:                 if (keyword != null && !vpcName.toLowerCase().contains(keyword.toLowerCase())) {
 161:                     continue;
 162:                 }
 163:                 List<Boolean> subList = subnetMap.get(vpc.vpcId());
 164:                 String blockPublicAccess = (subList != null && subList.stream().allMatch(b -> !b)) ? "Off" : "On";
 165: 
 166:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 167:                 item.put("vpcId", vpc.vpcId());
 168:                 item.put("name", vpcName);
 169:                 item.put("ownerId", vpc.ownerId());
 170:                 item.put("region", region);
 171:                 item.put("state", vpc.stateAsString());
 172:                 item.put("ec2Count", instanceCount.getOrDefault(vpc.vpcId(), 0));
 173:                 item.put("ipv4Cidr", vpc.cidrBlock());
 174:                 item.put("ipv6Cidr", vpc.ipv6CidrBlockAssociationSet().stream()
 175:                         .map(x -> x.ipv6CidrBlock()).toList());
 176:                 item.put("tenancy", vpc.instanceTenancyAsString());
 177:                 item.put("blockPublicAccess", blockPublicAccess);
 178:                 vpcs.add(item);
 179:             }
 180:         } catch (Exception e) {
 181:             log.error("Error get VPC on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 182:         }
 183:         return vpcs;
 184:     }

 203:     private List<Map<String, Object>> instancesForRegion(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 204:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 205:         try {
 206:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 207:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 208:                 return ec2s;
 209:             }
 210:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 211:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 212:             Set<String> vpcIdSet = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 213:             instances.forEach(i -> {
 214:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 215:                     vpcIdSet.add(i.vpcId());
 216:                 }
 217:             });
 218: 
 219:             Map<String, String> statusMap = instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 220:             Map<String, String> vpcNameMap = vpcNameMap(region, new ArrayList<>(vpcIdSet));
 221: 
 222:             for (Instance inst : instances) {
 223:                 if (vpcIds != null && (inst.vpcId() == null || !vpcIds.contains(inst.vpcId()))) {
 224:                     continue;
 225:                 }
 226:                 String vpcId = inst.vpcId() != null ? inst.vpcId() : "-";
 227:                 String name = inst.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 228:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 229: 
 230:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 231:                 item.put("instanceName", name);
 232:                 item.put("instanceId", inst.instanceId());
 233:                 item.put("instanceState", inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : "-");
 234:                 item.put("instanceType", inst.instanceTypeAsString() != null ? inst.instanceTypeAsString() : "-");
 235:                 item.put("statusCheck", statusMap.getOrDefault(inst.instanceId(), "-"));
 236:                 item.put("region", region);
 237:                 item.put("availabilityZone", inst.placement() != null ? inst.placement().availabilityZone() : "-");
 238:                 item.put("vpcName", vpcNameMap.getOrDefault(vpcId, vpcId));
 239:                 item.put("vpcId", vpcId);
 240:                 item.put("securityGroupName", inst.securityGroups().stream()
 241:                         .map(g -> g.groupName()).reduce((a, b) -> a + ", " + b).orElse("-"));
 242:                 // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?????몄뒪?댁뒪??硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕媛 ?녿뒗媛"瑜?紐⑸줉???듯븳??
 243:                 item.put("monitoredBy", monitoredBy(inst, region));
 244:                 ec2s.add(item);
 245:             }
 246:         } catch (Exception e) {
 247:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 248:         }
 249:         return ec2s;
 250:     }

 285:     public Map<String, Object> summaryInstances(List<String> regions) {
 286:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 287:         List<Callable<RegionInstances>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 288:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 289:             tasks.add(() -> instancesWithStatus(region));
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByRegion = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 293:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 294:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByState = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 295:         Map<String, Map<String, Integer>> ec2ByRegionAndStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 296: 
 297:         // AWS ?몄텧? 蹂묐젹, 吏묎퀎???쒖꽌 蹂댁〈 ?⑥씪 ?ㅻ젅??硫깅벑)濡??섑뻾.
 298:         for (RegionInstances ri : runParallel(tasks)) {
 299:             if (ri == null) {
 300:                 continue;
 301:             }
 302:             ec2ByRegion.merge(ri.region(), ri.instances().size(), Integer::sum);
 303:             ec2ByRegionAndStatus.put(ri.region(), new LinkedHashMap<>());
 304:             for (Instance inst : ri.instances()) {
 305:                 ec2ByType.merge(inst.instanceTypeAsString(), 1, Integer::sum);
 306:                 ec2ByState.merge(inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : null, 1, Integer::sum);
 307:                 String status = ri.statusMap().get(inst.instanceId());
 308:                 ec2ByRegionAndStatus.get(ri.region()).merge(status, 1, Integer::sum);
 309:             }
 310:         }
 311: 
 312:         Map<String, Object> items = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 313:         items.put("ec2ByRegion", ec2ByRegion);
 314:         items.put("ec2ByType", ec2ByType);
 315:         items.put("ec2ByState", ec2ByState);
 316:         items.put("ec2ByRegionAndStatus", ec2ByRegionAndStatus);
 317:         return Map.of("items", items);
 318:     }

 321:     @Override
 322:     public Map<String, Object> overview(List<String> regions, TimeRange timeRange) {
 323:         List<String> targets = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 324:         List<Callable<OverviewRegion>> tasks = targets.stream()
 325:                 .map(region -> (Callable<OverviewRegion>) () -> overviewForRegion(region)).toList();
 326:         List<OverviewRegion> parts = runParallel(tasks);
 327:         List<Map<String, Object>> collection = new ArrayList<>();
 328:         List<Map<String, Object>> risks = new ArrayList<>();
 329:         int healthy=0, warning=0, critical=0, unobserved=0, stopped=0, total=0;
 330:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 331:             if (p == null) continue;
 332:             collection.add(p.collection()); risks.addAll(p.risks());
 333:             total += p.total; healthy += p.healthy; warning += p.warning; critical += p.critical; unobserved += p.unobserved; stopped += p.stopped;
 334:         }
 335:         Map<String,Integer> coverage = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 336:         coverage.put("zabbix-agent", 0); coverage.put("cloudwatch", 0);
 337:         coverage.put("unavailable", 0); coverage.put("unmonitored", 0);
 338:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 339:             if (p != null) { p.coverage().forEach((k, v) -> coverage.merge(k, v, Integer::sum)); }
 340:         }
 341:         risks.sort((a,b) -> Integer.compare((int)b.get("rank"), (int)a.get("rank")));
 342:         risks.forEach(r -> r.remove("rank"));
 343:         Map<String,Object> summary = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 344:         summary.put("totalInstances", total); summary.put("healthyInstances", healthy); summary.put("warningInstances", warning);
 345:         summary.put("criticalInstances", critical); summary.put("unobservedInstances", unobserved); summary.put("stoppedInstances", stopped);
 346:         summary.put("activeAlarms", 0);
 347:         // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?먯씠?꾪듃 ?ㅼ튂?섎㈃ 硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕源뚯? 臾대즺" ?덈궡??洹쇨굅 ?섏튂.
 348:         summary.put("monitoringCoverage", coverage);
 349:         summary.put("lastCollectedAt", Instant.now().toString());
 350:         return Map.of("summary", summary,
 351:                 "collection", Map.of("regions", collection),
 352:                 "riskResources", risks.stream().limit(10).toList(),
 353:                 // instanceStates???ㅻ깄???щ즺 B)?먯꽌 ??AWS 異붽? ?몄텧 0. cpuTop/networkTop? 臾닿낵湲?
 354:                 // 紐⑤뱶?먯꽌 鍮꾩썙 ?붾떎(???몄뒪?댁뒪 吏???ъ븘?껋? ?덉궛???쒖슦??吏?.

 361:     private OverviewRegion overviewForRegion(String region) {
 362:         Instant collected = Instant.now();
 363:         List<Instance> instances;
 364:         Map<String,String> statuses;
 365:         try {
 366:             instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 367:             statuses = instanceStatuses(region, instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId).toList());
 368:         } catch (Exception e) {
 369:             return OverviewRegion.unavailable(region, "EC2_UNAVAILABLE");
 370:         }
 371:         List<Map<String,Object>> risks=new ArrayList<>();
 372:         Map<String,Integer> coverage=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 373:         int healthy=0,warning=0,critical=0,unobserved=0,stopped=0;
 374:         for (Instance i : instances) {
 375:             coverage.merge(monitoredBy(i, region), 1, Integer::sum);
 376:             String state=i.state()==null ? "unknown" : i.state().nameAsString(); String check=statuses.get(i.instanceId());
 377:             // ?먯젙 洹쒖튃? ?ㅻ깄???섏쭛(CloudSnapshotService)怨?怨듭쑀?쒕떎 ??蹂듭젣 湲덉?.
 378:             String severity = Ec2StatusRules.classify(state, check);
 379:             switch (severity) {
 380:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_STOPPED -> stopped++;
 381:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_CRITICAL -> critical++;
 382:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_WARNING -> warning++;
 383:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_UNOBSERVED -> unobserved++;
 384:                 default -> healthy++;
 385:             }
 386:             if (!"healthy".equals(severity)) { Map<String,Object> x=overviewInstance(i,region,check,severity); x.put("rank", "critical".equals(severity)?4:"warning".equals(severity)?3:"unobserved".equals(severity)?2:1); risks.add(x); }
 387:         }
 388:         Map<String,Object> ec2 = collectionState("ok", collected.toString(), null);
 389:         Map<String,Object> disabled = collectionState("disabled", null, "ZERO_COST_MODE");
 390:         return new OverviewRegion(Map.of("region",region,"ec2",ec2,"cloudWatch",disabled,"alarms",disabled), instances.size(),healthy,warning,critical,unobserved,stopped,risks,coverage);
 391:     }
 392:     private Map<String,Object> collectionState(String state, String lastCollectedAt, String reason) {
 393:         Map<String,Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("state", state); result.put("lastCollectedAt", lastCollectedAt); result.put("reason", reason); return result;
 394:     }

 398:         List<String> reasons=new ArrayList<>(); if(check!=null&&!"2/2 checks passed".equals(check))reasons.add("EC2_STATUS_CHECK_FAILED"); if(reasons.isEmpty())reasons.add("EC2_STATE_"+x.get("state")); x.put("reasons",reasons); return x;
 399:     }

 431:     @Override
 432:     public Map<String, Object> getEc2OperationalDetail(String instanceId, String region) {
 433:         try {
 434:             Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
 435:             if (instance == null) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INSTANCE_NOT_FOUND);
 436: 
 437:             Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
